<script>
'use strict';

/* ═══ tiny helpers ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
function esc(s) {
  return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
    return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
  });
}
function show(id) {
  ['scrSplash', 'scrSetup', 'scrLogin', 'scrApp'].forEach(function (s) {
    $(s).classList.toggle('hide', s !== id);
  });
}
var toastTimer = null;
function toast(msg, bad) {
  var t = $('toast');
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.style.background = bad ? 'var(--bad)' : 'var(--ink)';
  t.classList.remove('hide');
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(function () { t.classList.add('hide'); }, 3200);
}

var AVA_COLORS = ['#0281C2', '#7C3AED', '#0D9488', '#DB2777', '#D97706', '#4F46E5', '#059669', '#B91C1C', '#0369A1', '#9333EA'];
function avaColor(name) {
  var h = 0, s = String(name || '?');
  for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
  return AVA_COLORS[h % AVA_COLORS.length];
}
function initials(name) {
  var parts = String(name || '?').trim().split(/\s+/);
  return (parts[0][0] + (parts[1] ? parts[1][0] : '')).toUpperCase();
}
function avaHtml(name, sm, online) {
  return '<span class="ava' + (sm ? ' sm' : '') + '" style="background:' + avaColor(name) + '">' +
    esc(initials(name)) + (online ? '<span class="dot"></span>' : '') + '</span>';
}

function dkey(d) {
  return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
}
function todayStr() { return dkey(new Date()); }
function localDateOf(iso) {
  if (!iso) return '';
  var d = new Date(iso);
  return isNaN(d) ? '' : dkey(d);
}
function fmtNum(n) {
  var v = Math.round(Number(n) * 100) / 100;
  return isNaN(v) ? '0' : String(v);
}
function fmtWhen(iso) {
  if (!iso) return '';
  var d = new Date(iso); if (isNaN(d)) return '';
  var now = new Date();
  var time = d.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
  if (d.toDateString() === now.toDateString()) return time;
  var yd = new Date(now); yd.setDate(yd.getDate() - 1);
  if (d.toDateString() === yd.toDateString()) return 'Yesterday ' + time;
  return d.toLocaleDateString([], { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }) + ' ' + time;
}
function fmtDueChip(due, status) {
  if (!due) return '';
  var d = new Date(due + 'T12:00:00');
  var label = d.toLocaleDateString([], { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
  if (status === 'Done') return '<span class="chip">was due ' + label + '</span>';
  var t = todayStr();
  if (due < t) return '<span class="chip due-late">overdue — was due ' + label + '</span>';
  if (due === t) return '<span class="chip due-today">due today</span>';
  return '<span class="chip">due ' + label + '</span>';
}
function fmtAgo(iso) {
  if (!iso) return 'not signed in yet';
  var ms = Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime();
  if (isNaN(ms)) return '';
  if (ms < 150000) return 'here now';
  var m = Math.floor(ms / 60000);
  if (m < 60) return 'seen ' + m + ' min ago';
  var h = Math.floor(m / 60);
  if (h < 24) return 'seen ' + h + 'h ago';
  return 'seen ' + Math.floor(h / 24) + 'd ago';
}
function copyText(text) {
  if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
    return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function () { toast('Copied.'); },
      function () { fallbackCopy(text); });
  }
  fallbackCopy(text);
}
function fallbackCopy(text) {
  var ta = document.createElement('textarea');
  ta.value = text; ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0';
  document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select();
  try { document.execCommand('copy'); toast('Copied.'); } catch (e) { toast('Could not copy — long-press to copy it yourself.', true); }
  document.body.removeChild(ta);
}

/* ═══ transport: one function, two backends ══════════════════════════════ */
var HAS_GS = false;
try { HAS_GS = !!(window.google && google.script && google.script.run); } catch (e) {}

function rpc(action, data) {
  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
    if (HAS_GS) {
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(resolve)
        .withFailureHandler(function (err) { reject(new Error(err && err.message ? err.message : 'Network problem — trying again soon.')); })
        .api(Auth.token, action, data || {});
    } else if (window.FAKE_API) {
      // demo & tests: the real Code.gs running right here against a fake Google
      try { Promise.resolve(window.FAKE_API(Auth.token, action, data || {})).then(resolve, reject); }
      catch (e) { reject(e); }
    } else {
      reject(new Error('No server here. This file must run inside Google Apps Script.'));
    }
  });
}

/* ═══ state ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
var LSK = { token: '5cp2_token', chatSeen: '5cp2_chat_seen' };
// Real app: localStorage, so you stay signed in on your device.
// Demo & tests: sessionStorage, so every browser tab is its own "device".
var STORE = (function () {
  try { return window.FAKE_API ? window.sessionStorage : window.localStorage; }
  catch (e) { return { getItem: function () { return null; }, setItem: function () {}, removeItem: function () {} }; }
})();
var Auth = { token: '', me: null };
try { Auth.token = STORE.getItem(LSK.token) || ''; } catch (e) {}

var S = null;                 // last full pull
var lastV = -1;
var lastMsgHigh = '';         // newest message `created` we've already reacted to
var activeTab = 'tasks';
var chatChannel = 'ALL';
var chatSeen = {};
// Read marks belong to a person, not to a browser — otherwise the next person
// to sign in on the same laptop inherits them and never sees a chat badge.
function chatSeenKey() { return LSK.chatSeen + ':' + (Auth.me ? Auth.me.id : 'anon'); }
function loadChatSeen() {
  try { chatSeen = JSON.parse(STORE.getItem(chatSeenKey()) || '{}') || {}; } catch (e) { chatSeen = {}; }
}
var openComments = {};        // taskId -> true
var commentDrafts = {};       // taskId -> half-typed comment, kept across renders
var updDraft = null;          // half-filled daily update, kept across renders
var updEditing = false;       // re-opening today's update to change it
var updSelDay = '';           // '' = today (stays "today" across midnight)
var profDraft = null;         // question set being edited in its sheet
var pollAgain = false;
var sessionEpoch = 0;         // bumped on every sign-in/out; stale replies bail
var FAST = /[?&]fast/.test(location.search);
var POLL_VISIBLE = FAST ? 1200 : 12000;
var POLL_HIDDEN  = FAST ? 2500 : 45000;

function saveToken(t) {
  Auth.token = t || '';
  try { t ? STORE.setItem(LSK.token, t) : STORE.removeItem(LSK.token); } catch (e) {}
}
function personById(id) {
  if (!S) return null;
  for (var i = 0; i < S.people.length; i++) if (S.people[i].id === id) return S.people[i];
  return null;
}
function nameOf(id) {
  if (id === 'ALL') return 'Everyone';
  var p = personById(id);
  return p ? p.name : 'Someone who left';
}

/* ═══ audio: a ding, and a ring that will not be ignored ═════════════════ */
var Audio5 = {
  ctx: null,
  ensure: function () {
    try {
      if (!this.ctx) this.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    } catch (e) {}
    // A context created before the first tap exists but stays silent, so
    // "we have a context" is not the same as "sound will actually come out".
    return !!(this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'running');
  },
  tone: function (at, freq, dur, vol, type) {
    try {
      var o = this.ctx.createOscillator(), g = this.ctx.createGain();
      o.type = type || 'sine'; o.frequency.value = freq;
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, at);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol, at + 0.015);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, at + dur);
      o.connect(g); g.connect(this.ctx.destination);
      o.start(at); o.stop(at + dur + 0.05);
    } catch (e) {}
  },
  ding: function () {
    if (!this.ensure()) return;
    var t = this.ctx.currentTime + 0.02;
    this.tone(t, 880, 0.15, 0.22); this.tone(t + 0.13, 1318, 0.28, 0.18);
  }
};
var Ring = {
  on: false, nextAt: 0, iv: null,
  start: function () {
    if (this.on) return;
    if (!Audio5.ensure()) return;      // no gesture yet — overlay tap will retry
    this.on = true;
    this.nextAt = Audio5.ctx.currentTime + 0.05;
    var self = this;
    this.pump();
    this.iv = setInterval(function () { self.pump(); }, 400);
  },
  pump: function () {
    // schedule on the audio clock: keeps ringing even in throttled tabs
    if (!Audio5.ctx) return;
    var ahead = Audio5.ctx.currentTime + 2.6;
    while (this.nextAt < ahead) {
      var t = this.nextAt;
      Audio5.tone(t,        740, 0.16, 0.5, 'square');
      Audio5.tone(t + 0.20, 740, 0.16, 0.5, 'square');
      Audio5.tone(t + 0.42, 988, 0.34, 0.55, 'square');
      this.nextAt += 1.35;
    }
  },
  stop: function () {
    this.on = false;
    if (this.iv) { clearInterval(this.iv); this.iv = null; }
  }
};
document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function () {
  // The first tap anywhere is what lets the browser make sound. If a ring is
  // already waiting, start it the moment that happens.
  if (Audio5.ensure() && Alarm.current) { $('alHint').textContent = ''; Ring.start(); }
}, { capture: true });

function osNotify(title, body) {
  try {
    if (!('Notification' in window) || Notification.permission !== 'granted') return;
    if (!document.hidden) return;
    new Notification(title, { body: body, tag: '5cpulse' });
  } catch (e) {}
}
function askNotifyPermission() {
  try {
    if ('Notification' in window && Notification.permission === 'default') Notification.requestPermission();
  } catch (e) {}
}

/* ═══ alarm takeover ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
var Alarm = {
  current: null,
  answered: {},          // answered on this device, before the server confirms
  pending: function () {
    if (!S || !Auth.me) return [];
    var self = this;
    return S.messages.filter(function (m) {
      if (m.kind !== 'ring') return false;
      if (m.from_id === Auth.me.id) return false;
      if (m.to_id !== 'ALL' && m.to_id !== Auth.me.id) return false;
      if (self.answered[m.id]) return false;
      return !(m.acks || []).some(function (a) { return a.who === Auth.me.id; });
    });
  },
  check: function () {
    if (!inApp) return;
    var q = this.pending();
    if (!q.length) { this.clear(); return; }
    var m = q[0];
    var already = this.current && this.current.id === m.id;
    this.current = m;              // always the object from the LATEST pull
    if (already) { Ring.start(); return; }
    $('alFrom').textContent = nameOf(m.from_id) + (m.to_id === 'ALL' ? ' · to everyone' : '') + ' · needs an answer';
    $('alText').textContent = m.text;
    $('alHint').textContent = Audio5.ensure() ? (q.length > 1 ? (q.length - 1) + ' more waiting after this one' : '')
      : 'Tap anywhere once to turn the sound on';
    $('alarm').classList.remove('hide');
    Ring.start();
    osNotify('🔔 ' + nameOf(m.from_id) + ' needs you', m.text);
  },
  answer: function (ans) {
    var m = this.current;
    if (!m) return;
    Ring.stop();
    // Remembered by id, never by mutating the message: the next poll replaces
    // every message object, and the answer would be lost — the ring would
    // simply start again in the person's face.
    this.answered[m.id] = true;
    this.current = null;
    this.check();
    var self = this;
    rpc('ack', { messageId: m.id, answer: ans }).then(function (res) {
      if (!res.ok) {
        delete self.answered[m.id];
        toast(res.error || 'Could not send that answer.', true);
        self.check();
        return;
      }
      pollNow();
    }, function () {
      delete self.answered[m.id];
      toast('Could not reach the team — it will ring again.', true);
      self.check();
    });
    renderAll();
  },
  clear: function () {
    this.current = null;
    Ring.stop();
    $('alarm').classList.add('hide');
  }
};
$('alarm').addEventListener('click', function () {
  // any tap unlocks audio; if it wasn't running, start it now
  if (Audio5.ensure()) { $('alHint').textContent = ''; Ring.start(); }
});
Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#alarm .ans button'), function (b) {
  b.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    ev.stopPropagation();
    Alarm.answer(b.getAttribute('data-ans'));
  });
});

/* ═══ boot ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function boot() {
  if (window.FAKE_API) {
    $('demobar').classList.remove('hide');
    $('demoReset').addEventListener('click', function () {
      if (window.FAKE_RESET) window.FAKE_RESET();
      try {
        STORE.removeItem(LSK.token);
        for (var i = STORE.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
          var k = STORE.key(i);
          if (k && k.indexOf(LSK.chatSeen) === 0) STORE.removeItem(k);
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      location.reload();
    });
  }
  if (Auth.token) {
    rpc('pull', {}).then(function (res) {
      if (res.ok) { applyPull(res); enterApp(); return; }
      // Only a rejected token means "sign in again". Everything else is
      // Google having a moment, and signing the team out over that would be
      // the app losing their session for no reason.
      if (res.auth === false) { saveToken(''); startLogin(); return; }
      $('splashMsg').textContent = 'Google is slow right now — trying again…';
      setTimeout(boot, 4000);
    }, function () {
      $('splashMsg').textContent = 'Can’t reach Google right now — retrying…';
      setTimeout(boot, 3000);
    });
  } else {
    startLogin();
  }
}

function startLogin() {
  var stuck = function (msg) {
    show('scrSplash');
    $('splashMsg').textContent = msg;
    setTimeout(startLogin, 4000);      // never dead-end: keep trying
  };
  rpc('state', {}).then(function (res) {
    if (!res.ok) { stuck(res.error || 'Something went wrong — trying again…'); return; }
    if (res.setupNeeded) { show('scrSetup'); return; }
    renderLoginFaces(res);
    show('scrLogin');
  }, function (e) { stuck(String(e.message || e)); });
}

/* ═══ setup (first run) ══════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
$('suGo').addEventListener('click', function () {
  var name = $('suName').value.trim();
  if (!name) { $('suName').focus(); toast('Tell us your name first.', true); return; }
  $('suGo').disabled = true;
  rpc('setup', { company: $('suCompany').value.trim(), name: name, dept: $('suDept').value.trim() })
    .then(function (res) {
      $('suGo').disabled = false;
      if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error, true); if (/Already set up/.test(res.error || '')) startLogin(); return; }
      saveToken(res.token);
      Auth.me = res.me;
      showPinReveal(res.me.name, res.pin, true, function () {
        rpc('pull', {}).then(function (r2) {
          if (r2.ok) { applyPull(r2); enterApp(); }
          else { show('scrSplash'); boot(); }
        }, function () { show('scrSplash'); boot(); });
      });
    }, function (e) { $('suGo').disabled = false; toast(String(e.message || e), true); });
});

/* ═══ login ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
var loginPick = null;
var pinBuf = '';

function renderLoginFaces(state) {
  $('loginCo').textContent = state.company || '5 Circles';
  var html = '';
  state.people.forEach(function (p) {
    html += '<button class="face" data-id="' + esc(p.id) + '">' + avaHtml(p.name) +
      '<span class="nm">' + esc(p.name) + '</span></button>';
  });
  $('loginFaces').innerHTML = html;
  $('loginFaces').classList.remove('hide');
  $('loginPin').classList.add('hide');
  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('loginFaces').querySelectorAll('.face'), function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var p = null;
      state.people.forEach(function (x) { if (x.id === b.getAttribute('data-id')) p = x; });
      openPinPad(p);
    });
  });
}

function openPinPad(person) {
  loginPick = person;
  pinBuf = '';
  $('pinName').textContent = person.name;
  $('pinFace').innerHTML = avaHtml(person.name);
  $('pinErr').textContent = '';
  drawPinDots();
  $('loginFaces').classList.add('hide');
  $('loginPin').classList.remove('hide');
  if (!$('pinPad').children.length) buildPinPad();
}

function buildPinPad() {
  var keys = ['1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9', 'back', '0', 'del'];
  var html = '';
  keys.forEach(function (k) {
    if (k === 'back') html += '<button class="aux" data-k="back">‹ back</button>';
    else if (k === 'del') html += '<button class="aux" data-k="del">⌫</button>';
    else html += '<button data-k="' + k + '">' + k + '</button>';
  });
  $('pinPad').innerHTML = html;
  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('pinPad').querySelectorAll('button'), function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { pinKey(b.getAttribute('data-k')); });
  });
}

function pinKey(k) {
  Audio5.ensure();   // the taps double as the audio unlock gesture
  if (k === 'back') { startLogin(); return; }
  if (k === 'del') { pinBuf = pinBuf.slice(0, -1); drawPinDots(); return; }
  if (pinBuf.length >= 4) return;
  pinBuf += k;
  drawPinDots();
  if (pinBuf.length === 4) submitPin();
}

function drawPinDots() {
  var dots = $('pinDots').children;
  for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) dots[i].className = i < pinBuf.length ? 'on' : '';
}

function submitPin() {
  var pin = pinBuf;
  rpc('login', { personId: loginPick.id, pin: pin }).then(function (res) {
    if (!res.ok) {
      pinBuf = '';
      drawPinDots();
      $('pinErr').textContent = res.error || 'Wrong PIN.';
      $('loginPin').classList.add('shake');
      setTimeout(function () { $('loginPin').classList.remove('shake'); }, 400);
      return;
    }
    saveToken(res.token);
    Auth.me = res.me;
    rpc('pull', {}).then(function (r2) {
      if (r2.ok) { applyPull(r2); enterApp(); }
      else { show('scrSplash'); boot(); }
    }, function () { show('scrSplash'); boot(); });
  }, function (e) { $('pinErr').textContent = String(e.message || e); pinBuf = ''; drawPinDots(); });
}

$('pinForgot').addEventListener('click', function () {
  $('pinErr').textContent = 'Ask your admin — they can reset it from their Team tab.';
});

/* ═══ the app ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
var inApp = false;
var pollTimer = null;
var pollBusy = false;

function enterApp() {
  inApp = true;
  sessionEpoch++;
  loadChatSeen();
  show('scrApp');
  renderAll();
  Alarm.check();
  schedulePoll();
  startClock();
  askNotifyPermission();
}

/* "due today", "overdue" and "here now" are all read off the clock, so they
   have to be redrawn as time passes — not only when someone types something. */
var clockTimer = null;
var clockDay = todayStr();
function startClock() {
  clearInterval(clockTimer);
  clockTimer = setInterval(function () {
    if (!inApp || !S) return;
    var today = todayStr();
    if (today !== clockDay) { clockDay = today; renderAll(); return; }
    renderTeam();
    if (activeTab === 'tasks') renderTasks();
  }, 60000);
}

/** Presence that arrives on an otherwise-unchanged poll. */
function presenceSignature() {
  return S.people.map(function (p) { return p.id + fmtAgo(p.last_seen); }).join('|');
}
function applySeen(seen) {
  if (!S || !seen) return;
  var before = presenceSignature();
  S.people.forEach(function (p) {
    if (seen[p.id] && seen[p.id] > (p.last_seen || '')) p.last_seen = seen[p.id];
  });
  // Timestamps tick constantly; only redraw when the words on screen change,
  // or the Team tab would rebuild itself under the user's finger.
  if (activeTab === 'team' && presenceSignature() !== before) renderTeam();
}

function chatAtBottom() {
  if (activeTab !== 'chat') return true;
  var log = $('chatLog');
  if (!log) return true;
  // Someone reading back through history should stay where they are
  return (log.scrollHeight - log.scrollTop - log.clientHeight) < 80 ||
         (document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.scrollY - window.innerHeight) < 120;
}

function schedulePoll() {
  clearTimeout(pollTimer);
  pollTimer = setTimeout(pollNow, document.hidden ? POLL_HIDDEN : POLL_VISIBLE);
}

function pollNow() {
  if (!inApp) return;
  // A poll already on the wire cannot include the change just made, so
  // remember to go again the moment it lands instead of waiting a full round.
  if (pollBusy) { pollAgain = true; return; }
  pollBusy = true;
  var epoch = sessionEpoch;
  var finish = function () {
    pollBusy = false;
    if (epoch !== sessionEpoch || !inApp) return;   // signed out while in flight
    if (pollAgain) { pollAgain = false; pollNow(); return; }
    schedulePoll();
  };
  rpc('pull', { since: lastV }).then(function (res) {
    if (epoch !== sessionEpoch || !inApp) { pollBusy = false; return; }
    if (res.ok === false && res.auth === false) { pollBusy = false; signedOutElsewhere(); return; }
    if (res.ok && res.unchanged && res.seen) { applySeen(res.seen); }
    if (res.ok && !res.unchanged) {
      var stick = chatAtBottom();
      applyPull(res); renderAll(); Alarm.check();
      if (activeTab === 'chat' && stick) scrollChat();
    }
    finish();
  }, finish);
}

document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
  if (!document.hidden && inApp) pollNow();
});

function resetSessionState() {
  sessionEpoch++;
  S = null; lastV = -1; lastMsgHigh = '';
  chatChannel = 'ALL'; openComments = {}; commentDrafts = {};
  updDraft = null; updEditing = false; updSelDay = ''; profDraft = null;
  chatSeen = {}; pollAgain = false;
  Alarm.answered = {};
  Alarm.clear();
  clearInterval(clockTimer);
  // Nothing from the last person may be left hanging over the login screen
  ['veilTask', 'veilPin', 'veilAsk', 'veilProfile', 'veilPerson'].forEach(function (id) { $(id).classList.add('hide'); });
  if (askResolve) { askResolve(false); askResolve = null; }
  pinRevealDone = null;
}

function signedOutElsewhere() {
  inApp = false;
  clearTimeout(pollTimer);
  saveToken('');
  resetSessionState();
  toast('Please sign in again.');
  show('scrSplash');
  startLogin();
}

function applyPull(res) {
  var firstLoad = !S;
  res.updates = res.updates || [];    // an older Code.gs mid-upgrade sends neither
  res.profiles = res.profiles || [];
  S = res;
  lastV = res.v;
  Auth.me = res.me;

  // sounds for what's new since the last pull (never on the first one)
  var high = lastMsgHigh;
  var newestForMe = null;
  res.messages.forEach(function (m) {
    if (m.created > high) high = m.created;
    if (!firstLoad && m.created > lastMsgHigh && m.from_id !== Auth.me.id &&
        (m.to_id === Auth.me.id || m.to_id === 'ALL') && m.kind !== 'ring') {
      newestForMe = m;
    }
  });
  if (newestForMe) {
    Audio5.ding();
    osNotify(nameOf(newestForMe.from_id), newestForMe.text);
  }
  lastMsgHigh = high;
}

/* sign out */
$('meChip').addEventListener('click', function () {
  ask('Sign out of 5C Pulse on this device?', 'Sign out').then(function (yes) {
    if (!yes) return;
    rpc('logout', {}).then(function () {}, function () {});
    inApp = false;
    clearTimeout(pollTimer);
    saveToken('');
    resetSessionState();
    show('scrSplash');
    $('splashMsg').textContent = 'Signed out.';
    startLogin();
  });
});

/* tabs */
Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#tabs button'), function (b) {
  b.addEventListener('click', function () {
    activeTab = b.getAttribute('data-tab');
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#tabs button'), function (x) {
      x.classList.toggle('on', x === b);
    });
    $('tabTasks').classList.toggle('hide', activeTab !== 'tasks');
    $('tabUpdates').classList.toggle('hide', activeTab !== 'updates');
    $('tabChat').classList.toggle('hide', activeTab !== 'chat');
    $('tabTeam').classList.toggle('hide', activeTab !== 'team');
    $('fabTask').classList.toggle('hide', activeTab === 'chat' || activeTab === 'updates');
    if (activeTab === 'chat') { markChatSeen(); renderChat(); scrollChat(); }
    renderAll();
  });
});

/* ═══ render ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function renderAll() {
  if (!S || !inApp) return;
  $('hdCo').textContent = S.company;
  $('meChip').innerHTML = avaHtml(Auth.me.name, true) + '<span>' + esc(Auth.me.name.split(/\s+/)[0]) + '</span>';
  renderTasks();
  renderUpdates();
  renderChat();
  renderTeam();
  renderBadges();
}

function renderBadges() {
  var mine = S.tasks.filter(function (t) { return t.owner_id === Auth.me.id && t.status !== 'Done'; }).length;
  $('bdgTasks').textContent = mine;
  $('bdgTasks').classList.toggle('hide', mine === 0);
  // A quiet "!" until your own update for today is in
  var filedToday = !!updateFor(Auth.me.id, todayStr());
  $('bdgUpd').textContent = '!';
  $('bdgUpd').classList.toggle('hide', filedToday);
  var unread = countUnreadChat();
  $('bdgChat').textContent = unread > 99 ? '99+' : unread;
  $('bdgChat').classList.toggle('hide', unread === 0);
}

/* ── tasks tab ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function taskSort(a, b) {
  if ((a.stuck ? 0 : 1) !== (b.stuck ? 0 : 1)) return a.stuck ? -1 : 1;
  var ad = a.due || '9999', bd = b.due || '9999';
  if (ad !== bd) return ad < bd ? -1 : 1;
  return a.created < b.created ? -1 : 1;
}

function renderTasks() {
  // Keep whatever is half-typed, then carry it back into the fresh markup —
  // the board must stay live while someone is composing a comment.
  var ae = document.activeElement;
  var focusedTask = (ae && ae.getAttribute && ae.getAttribute('data-cmtin')) || null;
  var caret = focusedTask ? ae.selectionStart : 0;
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-cmtin]'), function (i) {
    commentDrafts[i.getAttribute('data-cmtin')] = i.value;
  });

  var mine = [], gave = [], rest = [];
  S.tasks.forEach(function (t) {
    if (t.owner_id === Auth.me.id) mine.push(t);
    else if (t.by_id === Auth.me.id) gave.push(t);
    else rest.push(t);
  });
  var html = '';
  html += taskSection('My work', mine, 'Nothing on your plate. Give yourself a task with the button below.');
  if (gave.length) html += taskSection('What I’ve given out', gave, '');
  if (Auth.me.role === 'Admin' && rest.length) html += taskSection('Everyone else', rest, '');
  if (!mine.length && !gave.length && !(Auth.me.role === 'Admin' && rest.length)) {
    html = '<div class="empty">No tasks yet.<br><b>+ Give a task</b> starts the first one.</div>';
  }
  $('taskSections').innerHTML = html;
  bindTaskEvents();

  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-cmtin]'), function (i) {
    var id = i.getAttribute('data-cmtin');
    if (commentDrafts[id]) i.value = commentDrafts[id];
    if (id === focusedTask) {
      i.focus();
      try { i.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (e) {}
    }
  });
}

function taskSection(title, list, emptyMsg) {
  var open = list.filter(function (t) { return t.status !== 'Done'; }).sort(taskSort);
  var done = list.filter(function (t) { return t.status === 'Done'; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return a.done_at < b.done_at ? 1 : -1; }).slice(0, 12);
  var html = '<div class="sect"><h2>' + esc(title) + (open.length ? ' <span class="n">' + open.length + '</span>' : '') + '</h2>';
  if (!open.length && !done.length) html += emptyMsg ? '<div class="empty">' + emptyMsg + '</div>' : '';
  open.forEach(function (t) { html += taskCard(t); });
  done.forEach(function (t) { html += taskCard(t); });
  return html + '</div>';
}

function taskCard(t) {
  var owner = nameOf(t.owner_id), giver = nameOf(t.by_id);
  var isParty = Auth.me.role === 'Admin' || t.owner_id === Auth.me.id || t.by_id === Auth.me.id;
  var cls = 's-' + (t.status === 'Doing' ? 'doing' : t.status === 'Done' ? 'done' : 'todo') + (t.stuck ? ' stuck' : '');
  var comments = S.messages.filter(function (m) { return m.task_id === t.id; });
  var html = '<div class="task ' + cls + '" data-id="' + esc(t.id) + '"><div class="bar"></div><div class="body">';
  html += '<div class="t">' + esc(t.title) + '</div>';
  if (t.note) html += '<div class="note">' + esc(t.note) + '</div>';
  html += '<div class="meta">' + avaHtml(owner, true) + ' <b>' + esc(t.owner_id === Auth.me.id ? 'Me' : owner) + '</b>';
  if (t.by_id !== t.owner_id) html += ' <span class="mut">· from ' + esc(t.by_id === Auth.me.id ? 'me' : giver) + '</span>';
  html += ' ' + fmtDueChip(t.due, t.status);
  if (t.stuck) html += ' <span class="chip stuckchip">🚩 stuck</span>';
  if (t.status === 'Done' && t.done_at) html += ' <span class="chip" style="background:var(--ok-soft);color:var(--ok)">done ' + esc(fmtWhen(t.done_at)) + '</span>';
  html += '</div>';
  if (isParty) {
    html += '<div class="ctrls"><span class="seg">';
    ['To do', 'Doing', 'Done'].forEach(function (st) {
      html += '<button data-st="' + st + '" class="' + (t.status === st ? 'on' + (st === 'Done' ? ' don' : '') : '') + '">' + st + '</button>';
    });
    html += '</span>';
    if (t.status !== 'Done') {
      html += '<button class="linkbtn alert' + (t.stuck ? ' on' : '') + '" data-act="stuck">' +
        (t.stuck ? '🚩 Stuck — tap when unstuck' : '🚩 I’m stuck') + '</button>';
    }
    html += '<button class="linkbtn" data-act="cmt">💬 ' + (comments.length || 'Comment') + '</button>';
    html += '<button class="linkbtn" data-act="edit">Edit</button>';
    html += '</div>';
  } else if (comments.length) {
    html += '<div class="ctrls"><button class="linkbtn" data-act="cmt">💬 ' + comments.length + '</button></div>';
  }
  if (openComments[t.id]) {
    html += '<div class="comments">';
    comments.forEach(function (m) {
      html += '<div class="cmt">' + avaHtml(nameOf(m.from_id), true) + '<div><span class="who">' +
        esc(nameOf(m.from_id)) + '</span> <span class="when">' + esc(fmtWhen(m.created)) + '</span><br>' +
        esc(m.text) + ackSummary(m) + '</div></div>';
    });
    if (isParty) {
      html += '<div class="crow"><input placeholder="Add a comment — it stays on record" maxlength="2000" data-cmtin="' +
        esc(t.id) + '"><button class="btn primary sm" data-act="cmtsend">Send</button></div>';
    }
    html += '</div>';
  }
  return html + '</div></div>';
}

function ackSummary(m) {
  if (!(m.acks || []).length) return '';
  var bits = m.acks.map(function (a) { return esc(nameOf(a.who)) + ': ' + esc(a.answer); });
  return '<div class="ackline">✔ ' + bits.join(' · ') + '</div>';
}

/** Runs an action once, keeping the button quiet until the server answers —
 *  a second tap on "Done" or "I'm stuck" would ring someone for nothing. */
function once(btn, run) {
  if (btn.disabled) return;
  btn.disabled = true;
  var free = function () { btn.disabled = false; };
  var p = run();
  if (p && p.then) p.then(free, free); else free();
}

function bindTaskEvents() {
  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('taskSections').querySelectorAll('.task'), function (card) {
    var id = card.getAttribute('data-id');
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(card.querySelectorAll('[data-st]'), function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () {
        once(b, function () {
          return rpc('setStatus', { taskId: id, status: b.getAttribute('data-st') })
            .then(afterWrite, writeFailed);
        });
      });
    });
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(card.querySelectorAll('[data-act]'), function (b) {
      var act = b.getAttribute('data-act');
      b.addEventListener('click', function () {
        if (act === 'stuck') {
          once(b, function () { return rpc('toggleStuck', { taskId: id }).then(afterWrite, writeFailed); });
        }
        if (act === 'cmt') { openComments[id] = !openComments[id]; renderTasks(); }
        if (act === 'edit') openTaskSheet(id);
        if (act === 'cmtsend') sendComment(card, id, b);
      });
    });
    var cin = card.querySelector('[data-cmtin]');
    if (cin) {
      cin.addEventListener('input', function () { commentDrafts[id] = cin.value; });
      cin.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) {
        if (ev.key === 'Enter' && !ev.repeat) card.querySelector('[data-act="cmtsend"]').click();
      });
    }
  });
}

function sendComment(card, id, btn) {
  var inp = card.querySelector('[data-cmtin]');
  var text = inp.value.trim();
  if (!text) { inp.value = ''; commentDrafts[id] = ''; return; }
  var t = S.tasks.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
  var other = t ? (t.owner_id === Auth.me.id ? t.by_id : t.owner_id) : 'ALL';
  var p = personById(other);
  if (other === Auth.me.id || !p || !p.active) other = 'ALL';   // they've left the team
  once(btn, function () {
    return rpc('send', { toId: other, text: text, taskId: id }).then(function (res) {
      if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'That didn’t send.', true); return; }
      inp.value = '';                 // cleared only once it is safely on record
      commentDrafts[id] = '';
      pollNow();
    }, writeFailed);
  });
}

function afterWrite(res) {
  if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'That didn’t work.', true); return; }
  pollNow();
}
function writeFailed(e) { toast(String(e.message || e), true); }

/* ── give / edit task sheet ────────────────────────────────── */
var sheetTaskId = null;   // null = new task
var sheetOwnerId = null;
var sheetTaskEditing = false;

$('fabTask').addEventListener('click', function () { openTaskSheet(null); });
$('tkCancel').addEventListener('click', function () { $('veilTask').classList.add('hide'); });

function openTaskSheet(taskId) {
  sheetTaskId = taskId;
  var t = taskId ? S.tasks.filter(function (x) { return x.id === taskId; })[0] : null;
  $('tkHead').textContent = t ? 'Change this task' : 'Give a task';
  $('tkGo').textContent = t ? 'Save changes' : 'Give the task';
  $('tkTitle').value = t ? t.title : '';
  $('tkNote').value = t ? t.note : '';
  $('tkDue').value = t ? t.due : '';
  $('tkRing').checked = false;
  sheetTaskEditing = !!t;
  sheetOwnerId = t ? t.owner_id : Auth.me.id;
  renderWhoGrid();
  $('veilTask').classList.remove('hide');
  if (!t) setTimeout(function () { $('tkTitle').focus(); }, 60);
}

function renderWhoGrid() {
  var html = '';
  S.people.filter(function (p) { return p.active; }).forEach(function (p) {
    var on = p.id === sheetOwnerId;
    html += '<button class="face" data-id="' + esc(p.id) + '" style="' +
      (on ? 'border-color:var(--brand); background:var(--brand-soft)' : '') + '">' +
      avaHtml(p.name) + '<span class="nm">' + esc(p.id === Auth.me.id ? 'Me' : p.name) + '</span></button>';
  });
  $('tkWho').innerHTML = html;
  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('tkWho').querySelectorAll('.face'), function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      sheetOwnerId = b.getAttribute('data-id');
      renderWhoGrid();
    });
  });
  // Nothing to ring when the task is your own
  $('tkRingRow').classList.toggle('hide', sheetTaskEditing || sheetOwnerId === Auth.me.id);
}

$('tkGo').addEventListener('click', function () {
  var title = $('tkTitle').value.trim();
  if (!title) { $('tkTitle').focus(); return; }
  $('tkGo').disabled = true;
  var done = function (res) {
    $('tkGo').disabled = false;
    if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'That didn’t work.', true); return; }
    $('veilTask').classList.add('hide');
    toast(sheetTaskId ? 'Saved.' : (sheetOwnerId === Auth.me.id ? 'Added to your list.' : 'Given to ' + nameOf(sheetOwnerId) + '.'));
    pollNow();
  };
  var fail = function (e) { $('tkGo').disabled = false; toast(String(e.message || e), true); };
  if (sheetTaskId) {
    var payload = { taskId: sheetTaskId, title: title, note: $('tkNote').value, due: $('tkDue').value };
    var cur = S.tasks.filter(function (x) { return x.id === sheetTaskId; })[0];
    // Only send the owner when it actually changed — a finished task belonging
    // to someone who has left must still be editable.
    if (!cur || sheetOwnerId !== cur.owner_id) payload.ownerId = sheetOwnerId;
    rpc('editTask', payload).then(done, fail);
  } else {
    rpc('createTask', { ownerId: sheetOwnerId, title: title, note: $('tkNote').value, due: $('tkDue').value, ring: $('tkRing').checked })
      .then(done, fail);
  }
});

/* ── chat tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function chatKey(m) {
  if (m.to_id === 'ALL') return 'ALL';
  return m.from_id === Auth.me.id ? m.to_id : m.from_id;
}
function chatMessages(channel) {
  return S.messages.filter(function (m) {
    if (m.task_id) return false;
    if (channel === 'ALL') return m.to_id === 'ALL';
    return (m.from_id === Auth.me.id && m.to_id === channel) ||
           (m.from_id === channel && m.to_id === Auth.me.id);
  });
}
function countUnreadChat() {
  var n = 0;
  S.messages.forEach(function (m) {
    if (m.task_id || m.from_id === Auth.me.id) return;
    if (m.to_id !== 'ALL' && m.to_id !== Auth.me.id) return;
    var k = chatKey(m);
    if (m.created > (chatSeen[k] || '')) n++;
  });
  return n;
}
function markChatSeen() {
  var high = chatSeen[chatChannel] || '';
  chatMessages(chatChannel).forEach(function (m) { if (m.created > high) high = m.created; });
  chatSeen[chatChannel] = high;
  try { STORE.setItem(chatSeenKey(), JSON.stringify(chatSeen)); } catch (e) {}
  renderBadges();
}

function renderChat() {
  if (!S) return;
  // channel chips
  var chips = '<button data-ch="ALL" class="' + (chatChannel === 'ALL' ? 'on' : '') + '">' +
    '<span class="ava sm" style="background:var(--brand)">All</span> Everyone' +
    (unreadDot('ALL') ? ' <span class="unread"></span>' : '') + '</button>';
  S.people.filter(function (p) { return p.active && p.id !== Auth.me.id; }).forEach(function (p) {
    chips += '<button data-ch="' + esc(p.id) + '" class="' + (chatChannel === p.id ? 'on' : '') + '">' +
      avaHtml(p.name, true) + ' ' + esc(p.name.split(/\s+/)[0]) +
      (unreadDot(p.id) ? ' <span class="unread"></span>' : '') + '</button>';
  });
  $('chatChips').innerHTML = chips;
  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('chatChips').querySelectorAll('button'), function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      chatChannel = b.getAttribute('data-ch');
      markChatSeen();
      renderChat();
      scrollChat();
    });
  });

  // messages
  var msgs = chatMessages(chatChannel);
  var html = '';
  if (!msgs.length) {
    html = '<div class="empty" style="margin-top:8px">' +
      (chatChannel === 'ALL' ? 'Say hello — messages here reach the whole team and stay on record.'
        : 'No messages with ' + esc(nameOf(chatChannel)) + ' yet.') + '</div>';
  }
  msgs.forEach(function (m) {
    if (m.kind === 'notice') {
      html += '<div class="sysline">' + esc(m.text) + ' · ' + esc(fmtWhen(m.created)) + '</div>';
      return;
    }
    var mine = m.from_id === Auth.me.id;
    html += '<div class="msg' + (mine ? ' mine' : '') + (m.kind === 'ring' ? ' ring' : '') + '">';
    if (!mine && chatChannel === 'ALL') html += '<div class="who" style="color:' + avaColor(nameOf(m.from_id)) + '">' + esc(nameOf(m.from_id)) + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="bub">' + (m.kind === 'ring' ? '<b>🔔 </b>' : '') + esc(m.text) + '</div>';
    html += ackSummary(m);
    html += '<div class="when">' + esc(fmtWhen(m.created)) + '</div></div>';
  });
  $('chatLog').innerHTML = html;

  // placeholder + ring visibility
  $('chatText').placeholder = chatChannel === 'ALL' ? 'Message everyone…' : 'Message ' + nameOf(chatChannel).split(/\s+/)[0] + '…';
  $('chatRing').classList.toggle('hide', chatChannel === 'ALL' && Auth.me.role !== 'Admin');
  if (activeTab === 'chat') markChatSeen();
}
function unreadDot(ch) {
  var seen = chatSeen[ch] || '';
  var msgs = chatMessages(ch);
  for (var i = 0; i < msgs.length; i++) {
    if (msgs[i].from_id !== Auth.me.id && msgs[i].created > seen) return true;
  }
  return false;
}
function scrollChat() {
  setTimeout(function () {
    var log = $('chatLog');
    if (log.lastElementChild) log.lastElementChild.scrollIntoView({ block: 'end' });
  }, 40);
}

var chatSending = false;
function sendChat(kind) {
  var text = $('chatText').value.trim();
  if (!text) { $('chatText').focus(); return; }
  if (chatSending) return;          // a held Enter must not post the line twice
  chatSending = true;
  $('chatSend').disabled = true;
  var done = function () { chatSending = false; $('chatSend').disabled = false; };
  rpc('send', { toId: chatChannel, text: text, kind: kind }).then(function (res) {
    done();
    if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'Could not send.', true); return; }
    $('chatText').value = '';
    pollNow();
    setTimeout(scrollChat, 300);
  }, function (e) { done(); writeFailed(e); });
}
$('chatSend').addEventListener('click', function () { sendChat('chat'); });
$('chatText').addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) {
  if (ev.key === 'Enter' && !ev.repeat) sendChat('chat');
});
$('chatRing').addEventListener('click', function () {
  var who = chatChannel === 'ALL' ? 'the whole team' : nameOf(chatChannel);
  if (!$('chatText').value.trim()) { toast('Write the message first — then ring.', true); $('chatText').focus(); return; }
  ask('Ring ' + who + '’s screen with this message? Their device takes over and keeps ringing until they answer.', 'Ring')
    .then(function (yes) { if (yes) sendChat('ring'); });
});

/* ── updates tab: everyone's minute on their day ───────────────
   Not another CRM: the CRM knows the customers, this knows the team.
   Each person answers the few questions that fit THEIR work (their
   question set), says how the day felt, and the whole team's day is
   one scroll — with the bell to chase whoever's word is missing.   */

var MOODS = {
  great: { e: '🔥', label: 'Great day' },
  ok:    { e: '🙂', label: 'Okay day' },
  heavy: { e: '😓', label: 'Heavy day' }
};

function profileById(id) {
  var list = (S && S.profiles) || [];
  for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (list[i].id === id) return list[i];
  return null;
}
function activeProfiles() {
  return ((S && S.profiles) || []).filter(function (p) { return !p.archived; });
}
/** The set a person answers — archived or missing falls back to General. */
function effectiveProfile(p) {
  var pr = profileById((p && p.profile_id) || 'general');
  if (!pr || pr.archived) pr = profileById('general');
  return pr || { id: 'general', name: 'General', emoji: '📝', questions: [] };
}
function updateFor(pid, date) {
  var us = (S && S.updates) || [];
  for (var i = us.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (us[i].person_id === pid && us[i].date === date) return us[i];
  }
  return null;
}
function updatesOn(date) {
  return ((S && S.updates) || []).filter(function (u) { return u.date === date; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return a.created < b.created ? 1 : -1; });
}
/** Days in a row with an update, counting back from the latest one.
 *  A missing Sunday is forgiven — the week off shouldn't cost the run. */
function streakFor(pid) {
  var have = {};
  ((S && S.updates) || []).forEach(function (u) { if (u.person_id === pid) have[u.date] = true; });
  var dates = Object.keys(have).sort();
  if (!dates.length) return 0;
  var d = new Date(dates[dates.length - 1] + 'T12:00:00');
  if (isNaN(d)) return 0;
  var n = 0;
  for (var guard = 0; guard < 400; guard++) {
    if (have[dkey(d)]) n++;
    else if (d.getDay() !== 0) break;
    d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  }
  return n;
}
function tasksDoneOn(pid, date) {
  return S.tasks.filter(function (t) {
    return t.owner_id === pid && t.status === 'Done' && localDateOf(t.done_at) === date;
  }).length;
}
function selDay() { return updSelDay || todayStr(); }
function fmtDayLabel(ds) {
  if (ds === todayStr()) return 'Today';
  var y = new Date(); y.setDate(y.getDate() - 1);
  if (ds === dkey(y)) return 'Yesterday';
  var d = new Date(ds + 'T12:00:00');
  return isNaN(d) ? ds : d.toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
}

function ensureUpdDraft() {
  if (updDraft) return updDraft;
  updDraft = { mood: '', vals: {}, note: '' };
  var mine = updateFor(Auth.me.id, todayStr());
  if (mine) {                       // changing today's — start from what's in
    updDraft.mood = mine.mood;
    updDraft.note = mine.note || '';
    var qs = effectiveProfile(Auth.me).questions || [];
    (mine.answers || []).forEach(function (a) {
      for (var i = 0; i < qs.length; i++) {
        if (qs[i].label === a.l && !(qs[i].id in updDraft.vals)) { updDraft.vals[qs[i].id] = a.v; break; }
      }
    });
  }
  return updDraft;
}

/** "3 videos" and "₹1500" read naturally; a unit already inside the question's
 *  own words ("Videos delivered") isn't said twice. */
function numChipHtml(v, unit, label) {
  var val = unit === '₹' ? '₹' + v : v;
  var showUnit = unit && unit !== '₹' &&
    String(label).toLowerCase().indexOf(String(unit).toLowerCase()) < 0;
  return '<span class="chip"><b>' + esc(val) + (showUnit ? ' ' + esc(unit) : '') + '</b>&nbsp;' + esc(label) + '</span>';
}

/** The chips + lines of one update — shared by the feed and the history sheet. */
function updBodyHtml(u) {
  var html = '';
  var nums = '';
  (u.answers || []).forEach(function (a) {
    if (a.t === 'count') {
      nums += numChipHtml(a.v, a.u, a.l);
    } else if (a.t === 'yesno') {
      nums += '<span class="chip">' + esc(String(a.l).replace(/\?\s*$/, '')) +
        ' — <b>' + (a.v === 'yes' ? 'yes' : 'no') + '</b></span>';
    }
  });
  var done = tasksDoneOn(u.person_id, u.date);
  if (done) nums += '<span class="chip" style="background:var(--ok-soft);color:var(--ok)">✅ ' + done + ' task' + (done === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' finished</span>';
  if (nums) html += '<div class="nums">' + nums + '</div>';
  (u.answers || []).forEach(function (a) {
    if (a.t === 'count' || a.t === 'yesno') return;
    html += '<div class="qa2"><div class="ql">' + esc(a.l) + '</div>' + esc(a.v) + '</div>';
  });
  if (u.note) html += '<div class="note">' + esc(u.note) + '</div>';
  return html ? '<div class="updb">' + html + '</div>' : '';
}

/** "Sales · ☎️ Sales" would be saying it twice — one label is enough. */
function whoDoesWhat(p, prof) {
  if (!p) return '';
  if (!prof) return p.dept;
  if (String(p.dept || '').toLowerCase() === String(prof.name).toLowerCase()) return prof.emoji + ' ' + prof.name;
  return (p.dept ? p.dept + ' · ' : '') + prof.emoji + ' ' + prof.name;
}

function updCard(u) {
  var p = personById(u.person_id);
  var name = nameOf(u.person_id);
  var prof = p ? effectiveProfile(p) : null;
  var mood = MOODS[u.mood] || null;
  var st = streakFor(u.person_id);
  var html = '<div class="upd"><div class="hd" data-pid="' + esc(u.person_id) + '">' + avaHtml(name, false) +
    '<div class="who"><div class="nm">' + esc(u.person_id === Auth.me.id ? 'Me' : name) + '</div>' +
    '<div class="what">' + esc(whoDoesWhat(p, prof)) +
    ' · ' + esc(fmtWhen(u.updated || u.created)) + '</div></div>';
  if (selDay() === todayStr() && st >= 2) html += '<span class="chip streak">🔥 ' + st + ' days</span>';
  if (mood) html += '<span class="mood" title="' + esc(mood.label) + '">' + mood.e + '</span>';
  html += '</div>' + updBodyHtml(u) + '</div>';
  return html;
}

function myUpdateHtml() {
  var today = todayStr();
  var mine = updateFor(Auth.me.id, today);
  var d = new Date();
  var dayWords = d.toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });

  if (mine && !updEditing) {
    var mood = MOODS[mine.mood];
    return '<div class="card updme"><div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px">' +
      '<div style="flex:1"><h3>Your update is in ✓</h3><div class="day">' + esc(dayWords) +
      (mood ? ' · ' + mood.e + ' ' + esc(mood.label) : '') + '</div></div>' +
      '<button class="btn ghost sm" id="updChange">Change it</button></div>' +
      updBodyHtml(mine) + '</div>';
  }

  var draft = ensureUpdDraft();
  var qs = effectiveProfile(Auth.me).questions || [];
  var html = '<div class="card updme"><h3>' + (mine ? 'Change today’s update' : 'Your day, in a minute') + '</h3>' +
    '<div class="day">' + esc(dayWords) + '</div>';
  html += '<div class="uq" style="margin-top:10px"><label>How was the day?</label><div class="moods">';
  ['great', 'ok', 'heavy'].forEach(function (m) {
    html += '<button data-mood="' + m + '" class="' + (draft.mood === m ? 'on' : '') + '">' +
      '<span class="e">' + MOODS[m].e + '</span>' + MOODS[m].label + '</button>';
  });
  html += '</div></div>';
  qs.forEach(function (q) {
    var v = draft.vals[q.id] !== undefined ? String(draft.vals[q.id]) : '';
    html += '<div class="uq"><label>' + esc(q.label) + '</label>';
    if (q.type === 'count') {
      html += '<div class="cnt"><input data-uq="' + esc(q.id) + '" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0" maxlength="12" value="' + esc(v) + '">' +
        (q.unit ? '<span class="unit">' + esc(q.unit) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    } else if (q.type === 'yesno') {
      html += '<span class="seg">' +
        '<button data-uq="' + esc(q.id) + '" data-v="yes" class="' + (v === 'yes' ? 'on' : '') + '">Yes</button>' +
        '<button data-uq="' + esc(q.id) + '" data-v="no" class="' + (v === 'no' ? 'on' : '') + '">No</button></span>';
    } else {
      html += '<input data-uq="' + esc(q.id) + '" placeholder="A line is enough" maxlength="300" value="' + esc(v) + '">';
    }
    html += '</div>';
  });
  html += '<div class="uq"><label>Anything else? <span class="mut" style="font-weight:400">(optional)</span></label>' +
    '<input data-uq="_note" placeholder="A win, a worry, a shout-out…" maxlength="1000" value="' + esc(draft.note) + '"></div>';
  html += '<div style="display:flex; gap:10px; margin-top:16px">' +
    (mine ? '<button class="btn ghost" id="updCancel">Cancel</button>' : '') +
    '<button class="btn primary block" id="updShare" style="flex:1">' +
    (mine ? 'Save the change' : 'Share my update') + '</button></div></div>';
  return html;
}

function renderUpdates() {
  if (!S || !inApp) return;
  // Keep half-typed answers alive across the poll's re-render, caret and all
  var ae = document.activeElement;
  var focusedQ = (ae && ae.getAttribute && ae.getAttribute('data-uq')) || null;
  var caret = focusedQ && ae.selectionStart != null ? ae.selectionStart : 0;

  $('myUpdate').innerHTML = myUpdateHtml();

  var today = todayStr();
  var chips = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
    var dd = new Date(); dd.setDate(dd.getDate() - i);
    var key = dkey(dd);
    var lbl = i === 0 ? 'Today' : i === 1 ? 'Yesterday'
      : dd.toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
    chips += '<button data-day="' + key + '" class="' + (selDay() === key ? 'on' : '') + '">' + esc(lbl) + '</button>';
  }
  $('updDays').innerHTML = chips;

  var day = selDay();
  var list = updatesOn(day);

  var pend = '';
  if (day === today) {
    var waiting = S.people.filter(function (p) { return p.active && !updateFor(p.id, today); });
    if (waiting.length) {
      var isAdmin = Auth.me.role === 'Admin';
      pend = '<div class="pendbar"><span>Still to come:</span>';
      waiting.forEach(function (p) {
        pend += '<span class="pendchip">' + avaHtml(p.name, true) + esc(p.id === Auth.me.id ? 'Me' : p.name.split(/\s+/)[0]) +
          (isAdmin && p.id !== Auth.me.id ? '<button class="ringbtn" data-nud="' + esc(p.id) + '" title="Ring them about it">🔔</button>' : '') +
          '</span>';
      });
      var others = waiting.filter(function (p) { return p.id !== Auth.me.id; });
      if (isAdmin && others.length > 1) {
        pend += '<span class="grow"></span><button class="btn ghost sm" id="nudAll">🔔 Ring them all</button>';
      }
      pend += '</div>';
    }
  }
  $('updPending').innerHTML = pend;

  var feed = '';
  if (!list.length) {
    feed = '<div class="empty">' + (day === today
      ? 'No updates yet today. Yours takes a minute — the card above is waiting.'
      : 'No updates came in on this day.') + '</div>';
  } else {
    list.forEach(function (u) { feed += updCard(u); });
  }
  $('updFeed').innerHTML = feed;

  bindUpdateEvents();

  if (focusedQ) {
    try {
      var inp = document.querySelector('#myUpdate [data-uq="' + CSS.escape(focusedQ) + '"]');
      if (inp && inp.tagName === 'INPUT') {
        inp.focus();
        inp.setSelectionRange(caret, caret);
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }
}

function bindUpdateEvents() {
  var box = $('myUpdate');
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(box.querySelectorAll('[data-mood]'), function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      ensureUpdDraft().mood = b.getAttribute('data-mood');
      renderUpdates();
    });
  });
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(box.querySelectorAll('input[data-uq]'), function (inp) {
    inp.addEventListener('input', function () {
      var d = ensureUpdDraft();
      var k = inp.getAttribute('data-uq');
      if (k === '_note') d.note = inp.value; else d.vals[k] = inp.value;
    });
  });
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(box.querySelectorAll('button[data-uq]'), function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var d = ensureUpdDraft();
      var k = b.getAttribute('data-uq'), v = b.getAttribute('data-v');
      d.vals[k] = d.vals[k] === v ? '' : v;    // tap again to un-answer
      renderUpdates();
    });
  });
  var change = $('updChange');
  if (change) change.addEventListener('click', function () { updEditing = true; updDraft = null; renderUpdates(); });
  var cancel = $('updCancel');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { updEditing = false; updDraft = null; renderUpdates(); });
  var share = $('updShare');
  if (share) share.addEventListener('click', function () {
    var d = ensureUpdDraft();
    if (!d.mood) { toast('How was the day? Tap one.', true); return; }
    once(share, function () {
      return rpc('saveUpdate', { date: todayStr(), mood: d.mood, vals: d.vals, note: d.note })
        .then(function (res) {
          if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'That didn’t go through.', true); return; }
          updDraft = null;
          updEditing = false;
          toast('Shared with the team. 👏');
          pollNow();
        }, writeFailed);
    });
  });

  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('updDays').querySelectorAll('button'), function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var day = b.getAttribute('data-day');
      updSelDay = day === todayStr() ? '' : day;
      renderUpdates();
    });
  });

  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('updPending').querySelectorAll('[data-nud]'), function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var pid = b.getAttribute('data-nud');
      ask('Ring ' + nameOf(pid) + '’s screen about today’s update?', 'Ring')
        .then(function (yes) { if (yes) sendNudge(pid); });
    });
  });
  var all = $('nudAll');
  if (all) all.addEventListener('click', function () {
    ask('Ring everyone whose update hasn’t come in? Their screens take over until they answer.', 'Ring them all')
      .then(function (yes) { if (yes) sendNudge(''); });
  });

  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('updFeed').querySelectorAll('.hd[data-pid]'), function (h) {
    h.addEventListener('click', function () { openPersonSheet(h.getAttribute('data-pid')); });
  });
}

function sendNudge(pid) {
  var data = { date: todayStr() };
  if (pid) data.personId = pid;
  rpc('nudgeUpdates', data).then(function (res) {
    if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'Could not ring.', true); return; }
    toast(res.nudged ? 'Ringing ' + res.nudged + (res.nudged === 1 ? ' phone…' : ' phones…')
      : 'Nothing to ring — already in, or already ringing.');
    pollNow();
  }, writeFailed);
}

/* ── one person's story: streak, week totals, recent updates ── */
function weekTotals(pid) {
  var start = new Date(); start.setDate(start.getDate() - 6);
  var startKey = dkey(start), endKey = todayStr();
  var totals = {}, order = [];
  ((S && S.updates) || []).forEach(function (u) {
    if (u.person_id !== pid || u.date < startKey || u.date > endKey) return;
    (u.answers || []).forEach(function (a) {
      if (a.t !== 'count') return;
      var k = JSON.stringify([a.l, a.u || '']);
      if (!(k in totals)) { totals[k] = 0; order.push(k); }
      totals[k] += parseFloat(a.v) || 0;
    });
  });
  return order.map(function (k) {
    var bits = JSON.parse(k);
    return { label: bits[0], unit: bits[1], total: totals[k] };
  });
}

function openPersonSheet(pid) {
  var p = personById(pid);
  if (!p) return;
  var prof = effectiveProfile(p);
  var st = streakFor(pid);
  var ups = ((S && S.updates) || []).filter(function (u) { return u.person_id === pid; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return a.date < b.date ? 1 : -1; }).slice(0, 14);
  var tot = weekTotals(pid);

  var html = '<div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px; margin-bottom:6px">' + avaHtml(p.name) +
    '<div><h3 style="margin:0">' + esc(p.name) + '</h3><div class="small mut">' +
    esc(whoDoesWhat(p, prof)) + '</div></div></div>';
  html += '<div style="display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:7px; margin:10px 0 4px">';
  if (st >= 2) html += '<span class="chip streak">🔥 ' + st + ' days running</span>';
  tot.forEach(function (t) {
    html += numChipHtml(fmtNum(t.total), t.unit, t.label + ' · 7 days');
  });
  html += '</div>';
  if (!ups.length) {
    html += '<div class="empty" style="margin-top:8px">No updates yet.</div>';
  }
  ups.forEach(function (u) {
    var mood = MOODS[u.mood];
    html += '<div class="upd"><div class="hd"><div class="who"><div class="nm" style="font-size:14px">' +
      esc(fmtDayLabel(u.date)) + '</div></div>' +
      (mood ? '<span class="mood">' + mood.e + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' + updBodyHtml(u) + '</div>';
  });
  html += '<button class="btn ghost block" id="vpClose" style="margin-top:8px">Close</button>';
  $('vpBody').innerHTML = html;
  $('vpClose').addEventListener('click', function () { $('veilPerson').classList.add('hide'); });
  $('veilPerson').classList.remove('hide');
}

/* ── question set editor (admins) ──────────────────────────── */
function openProfileSheet(pr) {
  profDraft = pr
    ? { profileId: pr.id, name: pr.name, emoji: pr.emoji,
        questions: (pr.questions || []).map(function (q) { return { id: q.id, label: q.label, type: q.type, unit: q.unit || '' }; }) }
    : { profileId: '', name: '', emoji: '', questions: [{ id: '', label: '', type: 'count', unit: '' }] };
  $('prHead').textContent = pr ? 'Edit — ' + pr.name : 'New question set';
  $('prName').value = profDraft.name;
  $('prEmoji').value = profDraft.emoji;
  renderProfQs();
  $('veilProfile').classList.remove('hide');
  if (!pr) setTimeout(function () { $('prName').focus(); }, 60);
}

function renderProfQs() {
  var html = '';
  profDraft.questions.forEach(function (q, i) {
    html += '<div class="qedit" data-i="' + i + '">' +
      '<input class="ql" placeholder="The question, in their words" maxlength="80" value="' + esc(q.label) + '">' +
      '<select class="qt">' +
      '<option value="count"' + (q.type === 'count' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Number</option>' +
      '<option value="text"' + (q.type === 'text' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Words</option>' +
      '<option value="yesno"' + (q.type === 'yesno' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Yes / No</option>' +
      '</select>' +
      (q.type === 'count' ? '<input class="qu" placeholder="unit" maxlength="12" value="' + esc(q.unit || '') + '">' : '') +
      (profDraft.questions.length > 1 ? '<button class="x" title="Remove this question">✕</button>' : '') +
      '</div>';
  });
  $('prQs').innerHTML = html;
  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('prQs').querySelectorAll('.qedit'), function (row) {
    var q = profDraft.questions[Number(row.getAttribute('data-i'))];
    row.querySelector('.ql').addEventListener('input', function (ev) { q.label = ev.target.value; });
    row.querySelector('.qt').addEventListener('change', function (ev) { q.type = ev.target.value; renderProfQs(); });
    var qu = row.querySelector('.qu');
    if (qu) qu.addEventListener('input', function (ev) { q.unit = ev.target.value; });
    var x = row.querySelector('.x');
    if (x) x.addEventListener('click', function () {
      profDraft.questions.splice(Number(row.getAttribute('data-i')), 1);
      renderProfQs();
    });
  });
}

$('prAddQ').addEventListener('click', function () {
  if (!profDraft) return;
  if (profDraft.questions.length >= 8) { toast('Eight questions is the most — keep it quick.', true); return; }
  profDraft.questions.push({ id: '', label: '', type: 'count', unit: '' });
  renderProfQs();
});
$('prCancel').addEventListener('click', function () { profDraft = null; $('veilProfile').classList.add('hide'); });
$('prSave').addEventListener('click', function () {
  if (!profDraft) return;
  profDraft.name = $('prName').value.trim();
  profDraft.emoji = $('prEmoji').value.trim();
  if (!profDraft.name) { $('prName').focus(); toast('Name the kind of work first.', true); return; }
  var btn = $('prSave');
  once(btn, function () {
    return rpc('saveProfile', profDraft).then(function (res) {
      if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'That didn’t save.', true); return; }
      profDraft = null;
      $('veilProfile').classList.add('hide');
      toast('Saved. It’s live for everyone on that set.');
      pollNow();
    }, writeFailed);
  });
});

/* ── team tab ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */
function renderTeam() {
  var admin = Auth.me.role === 'Admin';
  $('teamAdd').classList.toggle('hide', !admin);
  $('adminFoot').classList.toggle('hide', !admin);

  // A half-typed email must survive the poll's re-render, caret and all
  var ae = document.activeElement;
  var focusMail = (ae && ae.getAttribute && ae.getAttribute('data-mailin')) || null;
  var mailCaret = focusMail && ae.selectionStart != null ? ae.selectionStart : 0;
  var mailDrafts = {};
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-mailin]'), function (i) {
    mailDrafts[i.getAttribute('data-mailin')] = i.value;
  });

  if (admin) {
    if (document.activeElement !== $('chTime') && S.chaser) $('chTime').value = S.chaser;
    $('chOff').classList.toggle('hide', !S.chaser);
    $('chInfo').textContent = S.chaser
      ? 'on — every day at ' + S.chaser + ', whoever hasn’t sent that day’s update gets an email'
      : 'emails whoever hasn’t sent that day’s update — works even when nobody has the app open';
    $('openSheet').href = S.sheetUrl || '#';
    $('openSheet').classList.toggle('hide', !S.sheetUrl);
    if (document.activeElement !== $('coEdit')) $('coEdit').value = S.company;
    var depts = {};
    S.people.forEach(function (p) { if (p.dept) depts[p.dept] = 1; });
    $('deptList').innerHTML = Object.keys(depts).map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + esc(d) + '">'; }).join('');
    var keepProf = $('npProf').value;
    $('npProf').innerHTML = activeProfiles().map(function (pr) {
      return '<option value="' + esc(pr.id) + '">' + esc(pr.emoji + ' ' + pr.name) + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    var kept = keepProf && profileById(keepProf);
    if (kept && !kept.archived) $('npProf').value = keepProf;
    renderProfiles();
  }
  $('profSect').classList.toggle('hide', !admin);

  function profOptions(selId) {
    return activeProfiles().map(function (pr) {
      return '<option value="' + esc(pr.id) + '"' + (pr.id === selId ? ' selected' : '') + '>' +
        esc(pr.emoji + ' ' + pr.name) + '</option>';
    }).join('');
  }

  var html = '';
  S.people.filter(function (p) { return p.active; }).forEach(function (p) {
    var open = S.tasks.filter(function (t) { return t.owner_id === p.id && t.status !== 'Done'; }).length;
    var here = p.last_seen && (Date.now() - new Date(p.last_seen).getTime() < 150000);
    var st = streakFor(p.id);
    html += '<div class="pcard" data-id="' + esc(p.id) + '" style="cursor:pointer" title="See their updates">' +
      avaHtml(p.name, false, here) +
      '<div class="in"><div class="nm">' + esc(p.name) +
      (p.role === 'Admin' ? ' <span class="rolechip">ADMIN</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="sub">' + esc(p.dept) + ' · ' + open + ' open task' + (open === 1 ? '' : 's') +
      (st >= 2 ? ' · 🔥 ' + st : '') + '</div>' +
      (admin ?
        '<div class="padmin">' +
        '<select class="psel" data-act="prof" title="Their daily update questions">' + profOptions(effectiveProfile(p).id) + '</select>' +
        '<input class="pmail" data-mailin="' + esc(p.id) + '" placeholder="email for alerts" maxlength="120" inputmode="email" value="' +
          esc(mailDrafts[p.id] !== undefined ? mailDrafts[p.id] : (p.email || '')) + '">' +
        '<button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="mailsave">Save email</button>' +
        (p.id !== Auth.me.id ?
          '<button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="pin">Reset PIN</button>' +
          '<button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="role">' + (p.role === 'Admin' ? 'Make member' : 'Make admin') + '</button>' +
          '<button class="btn danger sm" data-act="rm">Remove</button>' : '') +
        '</div>' : '') +
      '</div><div class="presence' + (here ? ' on' : '') + '">' + esc(fmtAgo(p.last_seen)) + '</div></div>';
  });
  $('teamList').innerHTML = html;

  // The card itself opens that person's updates; buttons, pickers and inputs stay theirs
  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('teamList').querySelectorAll('.pcard'), function (card) {
    card.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
      if (ev.target.closest('button') || ev.target.closest('select') || ev.target.closest('input')) return;
      openPersonSheet(card.getAttribute('data-id'));
    });
  });

  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('teamList').querySelectorAll('[data-mailin]'), function (inp) {
    inp.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) {
      if (ev.key === 'Enter' && !ev.repeat) {
        var btn = inp.closest('.pcard').querySelector('[data-act="mailsave"]');
        if (btn) btn.click();
      }
    });
  });

  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('teamList').querySelectorAll('select[data-act="prof"]'), function (sel) {
    var pid = sel.closest('.pcard').getAttribute('data-id');
    sel.addEventListener('change', function () {
      rpc('editPerson', { personId: pid, profileId: sel.value }).then(afterWrite, writeFailed);
    });
  });

  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('teamList').querySelectorAll('button[data-act]'), function (b) {
    var card = b.closest('.pcard');
    var pid = card.getAttribute('data-id');
    var p = personById(pid);
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var act = b.getAttribute('data-act');
      if (act === 'mailsave') {
        var inp = card.querySelector('[data-mailin]');
        once(b, function () {
          return rpc('editPerson', { personId: pid, email: inp.value }).then(function (res) {
            if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'That didn’t save.', true); return; }
            toast(inp.value.trim() ? 'Saved — rings reach their inbox when they’re away from the app.' : 'Email removed.');
            pollNow();
          }, writeFailed);
        });
      }
      if (act === 'pin') {
        ask('Give ' + p.name + ' a brand-new PIN? Their old one stops working and they’re signed out.', 'Reset PIN')
          .then(function (yes) {
            if (!yes) return;
            rpc('resetPin', { personId: pid }).then(function (res) {
              if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error, true); return; }
              showPinReveal(p.name, res.pin, false, pollNow);
            }, writeFailed);
          });
      }
      if (act === 'role') {
        var toAdmin = p.role !== 'Admin';
        ask(toAdmin ? ('Make ' + p.name + ' an admin? They’ll be able to add and remove people.')
          : ('Make ' + p.name + ' a regular member?'), 'Change role')
          .then(function (yes) {
            if (yes) rpc('editPerson', { personId: pid, role: toAdmin ? 'Admin' : 'Member' }).then(afterWrite, writeFailed);
          });
      }
      if (act === 'rm') {
        ask('Remove ' + p.name + ' from the team? Their open tasks move to you so nothing is lost, and their history stays in the sheet.', 'Remove')
          .then(function (yes) {
            if (yes) rpc('removePerson', { personId: pid }).then(function (res) {
              if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error, true); return; }
              toast(p.name + ' removed.' + (res.movedTasks ? ' ' + res.movedTasks + ' open task' + (res.movedTasks === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' moved to you.' : ''));
              pollNow();
            }, writeFailed);
          });
      }
    });
  });

  if (focusMail) {
    try {
      var mi = document.querySelector('[data-mailin="' + CSS.escape(focusMail) + '"]');
      if (mi) { mi.focus(); mi.setSelectionRange(mailCaret, mailCaret); }
    } catch (e) {}
  }
}

/** The question sets themselves — the admin's shelf of "kinds of work". */
function renderProfiles() {
  var counts = {};
  S.people.forEach(function (p) {
    if (p.active) {
      var id = effectiveProfile(p).id;
      counts[id] = (counts[id] || 0) + 1;
    }
  });
  var html = '';
  activeProfiles().forEach(function (pr) {
    var used = counts[pr.id] || 0;
    var n = (pr.questions || []).length;
    html += '<div class="profcard" data-id="' + esc(pr.id) + '"><span class="em">' + esc(pr.emoji) + '</span>' +
      '<div class="in"><div class="nm">' + esc(pr.name) + '</div><div class="sub">' +
      n + ' question' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' · ' +
      (used ? 'answered by ' + used + (used === 1 ? ' person' : ' people') : 'nobody on it yet') +
      '</div></div>' +
      '<button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="edit">Edit</button>' +
      (pr.id !== 'general' && !used ? '<button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="arch">Archive</button>' : '') +
      '</div>';
  });
  $('profList').innerHTML = html;
  Array.prototype.forEach.call($('profList').querySelectorAll('[data-act]'), function (b) {
    var id = b.closest('.profcard').getAttribute('data-id');
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var pr = profileById(id);
      if (!pr) return;
      if (b.getAttribute('data-act') === 'edit') { openProfileSheet(pr); return; }
      ask('Put the ' + pr.name + ' set away? Updates already made with it stay readable; you can’t bring the set back, only make a new one.', 'Archive')
        .then(function (yes) {
          if (yes) rpc('archiveProfile', { profileId: id }).then(afterWrite, writeFailed);
        });
    });
  });
}
$('profNew').addEventListener('click', function () { openProfileSheet(null); });

$('npAdd').addEventListener('click', function () {
  var name = $('npName').value.trim();
  if (!name) { $('npName').focus(); return; }
  $('npAdd').disabled = true;
  rpc('addPerson', { name: name, dept: $('npDept').value.trim(), role: $('npRole').value, profileId: $('npProf').value, email: $('npMail').value })
    .then(function (res) {
      $('npAdd').disabled = false;
      if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error, true); return; }
      $('npName').value = '';
      $('npMail').value = '';
      showPinReveal(res.person.name, res.pin, false, pollNow);
    }, function (e) { $('npAdd').disabled = false; writeFailed(e); });
});
$('npName').addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) { if (ev.key === 'Enter') $('npAdd').click(); });

$('coSave').addEventListener('click', function () {
  rpc('setCompany', { company: $('coEdit').value.trim() }).then(afterWrite, writeFailed);
});
$('chSave').addEventListener('click', function () {
  var t = $('chTime').value;
  if (!/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(t)) { toast('Pick a time first.', true); $('chTime').focus(); return; }
  var bits = t.split(':');
  var d = new Date();
  d.setHours(Number(bits[0]), Number(bits[1]), 0, 0);
  if (d.getTime() <= Date.now()) d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
  once($('chSave'), function () {
    return rpc('setChaser', { epoch: d.getTime(), offsetMin: new Date().getTimezoneOffset(), label: t })
      .then(function (res) {
        if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'That didn’t set.', true); return; }
        toast('Set. Every day around ' + t + ', missing updates get an email.');
        pollNow();
      }, writeFailed);
  });
});
$('chOff').addEventListener('click', function () {
  rpc('clearChaser', {}).then(function (res) {
    if (!res.ok) { toast(res.error || 'That didn’t work.', true); return; }
    toast('Evening reminder off.');
    pollNow();
  }, writeFailed);
});
$('copyUrl').addEventListener('click', function () {
  copyText(appUrl());
});
function appUrl() {
  return (S && S.appUrl) || location.href.split('#')[0];
}

/* ── PIN reveal sheet ──────────────────────────────────────── */
var pinRevealDone = null;
function showPinReveal(name, pin, isSelf, then) {
  pinRevealDone = then || null;
  $('pvHead').textContent = isSelf ? 'Your PIN' : name + '’s PIN';
  $('pvWho').textContent = isSelf
    ? 'You’ll use this every time you sign in. Write it down now.'
    : 'Hand this to ' + name + ' privately — WhatsApp works.';
  $('pvPin').textContent = pin;
  $('pvNote').innerHTML = isSelf
    ? 'If you ever forget it, another admin can reset it — so add a second admin once your team grows.'
    : 'This is shown <b>only once</b>. If it gets lost, just reset it from the Team tab.';
  $('pvCopy').classList.toggle('hide', !!isSelf);
  $('pvCopy').onclick = function () {
    copyText('You’re on ' + ((S && S.company) || '5 Circles') + '’s 5C Pulse.\nOpen: ' + appUrl() +
      '\nTap your face → your PIN is ' + pin + '\n(Keep the PIN to yourself.)');
  };
  $('veilPin').classList.remove('hide');
}
$('pvOk').addEventListener('click', function () {
  $('veilPin').classList.add('hide');
  if (pinRevealDone) { var f = pinRevealDone; pinRevealDone = null; f(); }
});

/* ── confirm sheet ─────────────────────────────────────────── */
var askResolve = null;
function ask(text, yesLabel) {
  return new Promise(function (resolve) {
    askResolve = resolve;
    $('askText').textContent = text;
    $('askYes').textContent = yesLabel || 'Yes, do it';
    $('veilAsk').classList.remove('hide');
  });
}
$('askYes').addEventListener('click', function () { $('veilAsk').classList.add('hide'); if (askResolve) askResolve(true); askResolve = null; });
$('askNo').addEventListener('click', function () { $('veilAsk').classList.add('hide'); if (askResolve) askResolve(false); askResolve = null; });

/* veils close on backdrop tap */
['veilTask', 'veilPin', 'veilAsk', 'veilProfile', 'veilPerson'].forEach(function (id) {
  $(id).addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    if (ev.target !== $(id)) return;
    if (id === 'veilPin') return;      // the PIN sheet closes only on "Done"
    $(id).classList.add('hide');
    if (id === 'veilAsk' && askResolve) { askResolve(false); askResolve = null; }
    if (id === 'veilProfile') profDraft = null;
  });
});

/* ═══ go ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
show('scrSplash');
boot();
</script>
</body>
</html>

4Put it online

Top right: Deploy›New deployment. Next to “Select type”, press the gear and choose Web app. Then set:

Execute as: Me  ·  Who has access: Anyone

Press Deploy. Google will ask you to Authorize access — pick your account.

You’ll see a warning that Google hasn’t verified this app. That’s normal — it means “this is your own private script, not an app from the store”. Click Advanced, then Go to 5C Pulse (unsafe), then Allow.

“Who has access: Anyone” only means the login screen is reachable — every screen beyond it needs a PIN.

5That address is your team’s tool

Google shows a Web app URL ending in /exec. Copy it and keep it — that link is 5C Pulse for your whole team, on every device.

Open it. You’ll get the welcome screen you just tried in the demo: type the company name and your own name, and the tool makes you the admin and hands you your PIN.

Write your PIN down the moment you see it. It’s shown once. (If you ever lose it, any other admin can reset it — so make a second admin once the team is in.)

6Invite the team from your phone, one name at a time

In the app: Team›Add someone. Each person gets their own PIN, shown to you once, with a Copy invite message button — it writes the WhatsApp message for you: the link, their face, their PIN.

They open the link on their phone, tap their face, type the PIN — that’s the whole training.

Good to know

Questions you’ll have on day two

Where exactly is our data?

In a spreadsheet called “5C Pulse — Team Data” in the Google Drive of the account that did the setup. The Team tab has an Open the Google Sheet button. Every person, task, daily update, message and answer is a row there — it’s your permanent record, and you can read it like any sheet.

We already have a CRM — why this?

The CRM knows your customers. This knows your team. Every evening each person spends one minute on their own questions — the video editor on videos delivered, the marketer on leads and spend, sales on calls and closures, the mentor on sessions, the front desk on walk-ins — plus how the day felt and anything they want to say. You open the Updates tab and read the whole company’s day in one scroll, with streaks and weekly totals per person. No role is hard-coded: the starter question sets are yours to reword in the Team tab, and a brand-new kind of work gets its own set in a minute — so the tool fits whatever team you run next.

Someone keeps “forgetting” their update.

The Updates tab shows who’s still to come. Tap the 🔔 next to their name (or Ring them all) and their screen takes over and keeps ringing until they answer — the same bell as everything else, on record like everything else. And in the Team tab you can set an evening reminder: every day at your chosen time, whoever hasn’t sent that day’s update gets an email — it runs on Google’s side even when nobody has the app open.

How does the bell work? What if their tab is closed?

A ring takes over the other person’s screen and keeps sounding until they pick an answer — and their answer comes back to you on the task, on record. It rings as long as they have the page open (tell the team: keep the tab open during working hours, volume up). If their tab is closed, the ring waits for their next visit — and if you’ve put their email on their Team-tab card, it also lands in their inbox right away, sent from your own Gmail, so their phone still buzzes. At most one email per person every few minutes, and a ring already showing on their screen sends none.

Someone forgot their PIN.

Team tab → their card → Reset PIN. They’re signed out everywhere and you get a fresh PIN to hand them, shown once.

What if a stranger finds our link?

They see the login faces and nothing else — no tasks, no messages, no way in without a PIN. Five wrong guesses locks that person’s login for five minutes. Still, treat the link like the office keys: share it only with the team.

I want to change the tool later.

Paste the newer file over the old one in the editor, then Deploy → Manage deployments → ✏️ → Version: New version → Deploy. The address stays exactly the same — nobody has to re-bookmark anything. If a new version brings new abilities (like sending email), Google asks for permission once more: in the editor pick the function authorizeUpgrade, press Run, and click through Advanced → Allow like on day one — then make the new version.

What does it cost?

Nothing. It runs inside the free allowance of your own Google account, sized far beyond what a small team’s taps and messages will ever use.

Made for 5 Circles · The same two files also sit in your handover folder as Code.gs and index.html
